   522	
   523	// ============================================================================
   524	// Room Response Simulation State
   525	// ============================================================================
   526	
   527	struct SimulationState {
   528	    size_t numTriangles;
   529	    size_t numTimesteps;
   530	
   531	    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_WORLD;
   532	    int rank = 0;
   533	    int numRanks = 1;
   534	    size_t firstTriangle = 0;
   535	    size_t localTriangleCount = 0;
   536	    std::vector<int> triangleCounts;
   537	    std::vector<int> triangleDisplacements;
   538	
   539	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   540	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   541	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   542	    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // This rank's contiguous rows of Kij
   543	    std::vector<delay_t> tau;       // This rank's contiguous rows of Tau
   544	    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Replicated completed radiosity (T x N)
   545	    std::vector<val_t> localDistances;
   546	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Complete result on rank zero only
   547	
   548	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   549	
   550	    size_t sourceIndex;
   551	    int minimumDelay = 1;
   552	
   553	    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
   554	    size_t idxLocal(size_t localI, size_t j) const { return localI * numTriangles + j; }
   555	    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
   556	};
   557	
   558	// ============================================================================
   559	// Initialization
   560	// ============================================================================
   561	
   562	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   563	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
   564	    // Generate mesh
   565	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   566	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   567	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   568	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   569	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   570	
   571	    if (state.isRoot()) {
   572	        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   573	    }
   574	
   575	    // Contiguous, nearly equal row blocks make all timestep exchanges a single
   576	    // Allgatherv and keep each rank's O(N^2/P) matrices cache-friendly.
   577	    state.triangleCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks));
   578	    state.triangleDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks));
   579	    const size_t rowsPerRank = state.numTriangles / static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks);
   580	    const size_t extraRows = state.numTriangles % static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks);
   581	    size_t displacement = 0;
   582	    for (int r = 0; r < state.numRanks; ++r) {
   583	        const size_t count = rowsPerRank + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
   584	        state.triangleCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = static_cast<int>(count);
   585	        state.triangleDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
   586	        displacement += count;
   587	    }
   588	    state.firstTriangle = static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(state.rank)]);
   589	    state.localTriangleCount = static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleCounts[static_cast<size_t>(state.rank)]);
   590	
   591	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   592	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("Building octree...\n");
   593	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   594	
   595	    // Initialize areas
   596	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   597	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   598	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   599	    }
   600	
   601	    // Initialize reflectivity
   602	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   603	
   604	    // Initialize matrices
   605	    state.kij.resize(state.localTriangleCount * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   606	    state.tau.resize(state.localTriangleCount * state.numTriangles, 0);
   607	    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   608	    state.localDistances.resize(state.localTriangleCount, ZERO);
   609	    if (state.isRoot()) state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   610	}
   611	
   612	// ============================================================================
   613	// Precomputation Phase
   614	// ============================================================================
   615	
   616	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   617	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   618	    RandomGenerator rng(42);
   619	
   620	    // The sequential benchmark owns one global mt19937 stream. Position each
   621	    // rank at the start of its row block so the samples, and therefore results,
   622	    // remain bit-for-bit independent of the number of MPI ranks.
   623	    uint64_t precedingRandomSamples = 0;
   624	    if (state.localTriangleCount != 0) {
   625	        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.firstTriangle; ++i) {
   626	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   627	                if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
   628	                    precedingRandomSamples += 2ULL * NUM_RAYS;
   629	                }
   630	            }
   631	        }
   632	        rng.discard(precedingRandomSamples);
   633	    }
   634	
   635	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangleCount; ++localI) {
   636	        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   637	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   638	            if (i == j) continue;
   639	            state.kij[state.idxLocal(localI, j)] = computeKij(
   640	                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   641	        }
   642	    }
   643	
   644	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("  Form-factor rows distributed across %d rank(s)\n", state.numRanks);
   645	}
   646	
   647	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   648	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   649	
   650	    int localMinimumDelay = INT_MAX;
   651	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangleCount; ++localI) {
   652	        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   653	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   654	            if (i == j) continue;
   655	            const int delay = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   656	            // Triangle centers lie inside a radius-10 sphere, so Tau <= 40.
   657	            state.tau[state.idxLocal(localI, j)] = static_cast<delay_t>(delay);
   658	            localMinimumDelay = std::min(localMinimumDelay, delay);
   659	        }
   660	    }
   661	
   662	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMinimumDelay, &state.minimumDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, state.comm);
   663	    state.minimumDelay = std::max(state.minimumDelay, 1);
   664	}
   665	
   666	// ============================================================================
   667	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   668	// ============================================================================
   669	
   670	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   671	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   672	
   673	    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
   674	    const size_t localN = state.localTriangleCount;
   675	    const size_t timeOff = state.numTimesteps / 2;
   676	
   677	    // All non-self dependencies are delayed by at least minimumDelay. Compute
   678	    // that many timesteps at once, then exchange them together. This preserves
   679	    // the original recurrence while amortizing collective latency on coarse
   680	    // meshes. Keep MPI's int displacements representable for unusual inputs.
   680	    // meshes. Keep MPI's int displacements representable for unusual inputs.
   681	    const size_t maximumMpiBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / n);
   682	    const size_t preferredBlock = std::max<size_t>(
   683	        1, std::min(static_cast<size_t>(state.minimumDelay), maximumMpiBlock));
   684	
   685	    std::vector<val_t> localBlock;
   686	    std::vector<val_t> gatheredBlock;
   687	    std::vector<int> blockCounts(static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks));
   688	    std::vector<int> blockDisplacements(static_cast<size_t>(state.numRanks));
   689	
   690	    for (size_t blockStart = 0; blockStart < state.numTimesteps; blockStart += preferredBlock) {
   691	        const size_t blockLength = std::min(preferredBlock, state.numTimesteps - blockStart);
   692	
   693	        if (blockLength == 1) {
   694	            const size_t t = blockStart;
   695	            val_t* current = state.radB.data() + state.idxTN(t, 0);
   696	
   697	            for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localN; ++localI) {
   698	                const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   699	                const val_t* const kijRow = state.kij.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   700	                const delay_t* const tauRow = state.tau.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   701	                val_t sumB = ZERO;
   702	
   703	                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   704	                    if (i == j) continue;
   705	                    const int tauij = static_cast<int>(tauRow[j]);
   706	                    if (t < static_cast<size_t>(tauij)) continue;
   707	
   708	                    const val_t kij = kijRow[j];
   709	                    if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   710	                    const val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij), j)];
   711	                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   712	                    sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   713	                }
   714	
   715	                const val_t emission = (i == state.sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
   716	                current[i] = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
   717	            }
   718	
   719	            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_FLOAT,
   720	                           current, state.triangleCounts.data(),
   721	                           state.triangleDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, state.comm);
   722	            continue;
   723	        }
   724	
   725	        localBlock.assign(blockLength * localN, ZERO);
   726	        gatheredBlock.resize(blockLength * n);
   727	
   728	        for (size_t dt = 0; dt < blockLength; ++dt) {
   729	            const size_t t = blockStart + dt;
   730	            val_t* const localCurrent = localBlock.data() + dt * localN;
   731	
   732	            for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localN; ++localI) {
   733	                const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   734	                const val_t* const kijRow = state.kij.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   735	                const delay_t* const tauRow = state.tau.data() + state.idxLocal(localI, 0);
   736	                val_t sumB = ZERO;
   737	
   738	                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   739	                    if (i == j) continue;
   740	                    const int tauij = static_cast<int>(tauRow[j]);
   741	                    if (t < static_cast<size_t>(tauij)) continue;
   742	
   743	                    const val_t kij = kijRow[j];
   744	                    if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   745	                    const val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij), j)];
   746	                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   747	                    sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   748	                }
   749	
   750	                const val_t emission = (i == state.sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
   751	                localCurrent[localI] = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
   752	            }
   753	        }
   754	
   755	        int displacement = 0;
   756	        for (int r = 0; r < state.numRanks; ++r) {
   757	            blockCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = static_cast<int>(
   758	                blockLength * static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)]));
   759	            blockDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = displacement;
   760	            displacement += blockCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)];
   761	        }
   762	
   763	        MPI_Allgatherv(localBlock.data(), static_cast<int>(localBlock.size()), MPI_FLOAT,
   764	                       gatheredBlock.data(), blockCounts.data(), blockDisplacements.data(),
   765	                       MPI_FLOAT, state.comm);
   766	
   767	        // The collective returns rank-major blocks; transpose those small
   768	        // blocks into the time-major history used by the numerical kernel.
   769	        for (int r = 0; r < state.numRanks; ++r) {
   770	            const size_t rankN = static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)]);
   771	            const size_t rankFirst = static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(r)]);
   772	            const size_t packedFirst = static_cast<size_t>(blockDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(r)]);
   773	            for (size_t dt = 0; dt < blockLength; ++dt) {
   774	                std::copy_n(gatheredBlock.data() + packedFirst + dt * rankN, rankN,
   775	                            state.radB.data() + state.idxTN(blockStart + dt, rankFirst));
   776	            }
   777	        }
   778	    }
   779	
   780	    if (state.isRoot()) {
   781	        printf("  Completed %zu timestep(s), exchanging up to %zu at once\n",
   782	               state.numTimesteps, preferredBlock);
   783	    }
   784	}
   785	
   786	// ============================================================================
   787	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   788	// ============================================================================
   789	
   790	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   791	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   792	
   793	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangleCount; ++localI) {
   794	        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   795	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   796	        int bestT = 0;
   797	
   798	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   799	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   800	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   801	
   802	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   803	                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
   804	                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
   805	                sum += pS * pB;
   806	            }
   807	
   808	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   809	                maxCorr = sum;
   810	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   811	            }
   812	        }
   813	
   814	        state.localDistances[localI] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   815	    }
   816	
   817	    MPI_Gatherv(state.localDistances.data(), static_cast<int>(state.localTriangleCount), MPI_FLOAT,
   818	                state.isRoot() ? state.distances.data() : nullptr,
   819	                state.triangleCounts.data(), state.triangleDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
   820	                0, state.comm);
   821	}
   822	
   823	// ============================================================================
   824	// Validation
   825	// ============================================================================
   826	
   827	bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
   828	    int localReceivedEnergy = 0;
   829	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangleCount; ++localI) {
   830	        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
   831	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   832	            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
   833	                localReceivedEnergy++;
   834	                break;
   835	            }
   836	        }
   837	    }
   838	
   839	    unsigned long long localNonZeroKij = 0;
   840	    for (const val_t value : state.kij) {
   841	        if (value > EPSILON) ++localNonZeroKij;
   842	    }
   843	
   844	    int receivedEnergy = 0;
   845	    unsigned long long nonZeroKij = 0;
   846	    MPI_Reduce(&localReceivedEnergy, &receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
   847	    MPI_Reduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   848	               MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
   849	
   850	    int valid = 1;
   851	    if (state.isRoot()) {
   852	        printf("\nValidation:\n");
   853	
   854	        bool allNonNegative = true;
   855	        val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   856	        val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   857	        val_t sumDist = ZERO;
   858	        int nonZeroCount = 0;
   859	
   860	        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   861	            const val_t d = state.distances[i];
   862	            if (d < ZERO) {
   863	                allNonNegative = false;
   864	                printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   865	            }
   866	            if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
   867	                valid = 0;
   868	                printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   869	            }
   870	            minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
   871	            maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
   872	            sumDist += d;
   873	            if (d > EPSILON) ++nonZeroCount;
   874	        }
   875	
   876	        printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
   877	        printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
   878	        printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);
   879	
   880	        const val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
   881	        if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
   882	            printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
   883	        }
   884	
   885	        printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);
   886	        if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
   887	            printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
   888	            valid = 0;
   889	        }
   890	
   891	        const size_t matrixElements = state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles;
   892	        printf("  Non-zero form factors: %llu/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
   893	               nonZeroKij, matrixElements,
   894	               100.0 * static_cast<double>(nonZeroKij) / static_cast<double>(matrixElements));
   895	        if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
   896	            printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
   897	            valid = 0;
   898	        }
   899	
   900	        if (!allNonNegative) valid = 0;
   901	        if (valid) printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   902	    }
   903	
   904	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, state.comm);
   905	    return valid != 0;
   906	}
   907	
   908	// ============================================================================
   909	// Hash for Verification
   910	// ============================================================================
   911	
   912	uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
   913	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   914	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   915	        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
   916	        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   917	    }
   918	    return hash;
   919	}

   922	// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
   923	// ============================================================================
   924	
   925	int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
   950	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   951	    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
   952	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   953	}
   954	
   955	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   956	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   957	
   958	    int rank = 0;
   959	    int numRanks = 1;
   960	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   961	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);
   962	
   963	    int targetTriangles = 320;
   964	    int timesteps = 50;
   965	    int sourceIdx = 0;
   966	    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
   967	    bool validate = false;
   968	    bool printResults = false;
   969	
   970	    // Parse command line arguments
   971	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   972	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   973	            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
   974	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   975	            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   976	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   977	            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   978	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   979	            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
   980	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   981	            validate = true;
   982	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
   983	            printResults = true;
   984	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   985	            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
   986	            MPI_Finalize();
   987	            return 0;
   988	        } else {
   989	            if (rank == 0) {
   990	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   991	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   992	            }
   993	            MPI_Finalize();
   994	            return 1;
   995	        }
   996	    }
   997	
   998	    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
   999	
  1000	    if (rank == 0) {
  1001	        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
  1002	        printf("===================================\n");
  1003	        printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", numRanks);
  1004	        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
  1005	        printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
  1006	        printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
  1007	        printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
  1008	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
  1009	        printf("\n");
  1010	    }
  1011	
  1012	    // Initialize
  1013	    SimulationState state;
  1014	    state.rank = rank;
  1015	    state.numRanks = numRanks;
  1016	    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
  1017	                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
  1018	
  1019	    if (state.isRoot()) printf("\n");
  1020	
  1021	    // Precomputation
  1022	    MPI_Barrier(state.comm);
  1023	    const double startPre = MPI_Wtime();
  1024	
  1025	    computeTimeDelays(state);
  1026	    computeFormFactors(state);
  1027	
  1028	    const double localPreDuration = (MPI_Wtime() - startPre) * 1000.0;
  1029	    double preDurationMs = 0.0;
  1030	    MPI_Reduce(&localPreDuration, &preDurationMs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, state.comm);
  1031	    const long preDuration = static_cast<long>(preDurationMs);
  1032	
  1033	    if (state.isRoot()) {
  1034	        printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
  1035	        printf("\n");
  1036	    }
  1037	
  1038	    // Simulation
  1039	    MPI_Barrier(state.comm);
  1040	    const double startSim = MPI_Wtime();
  1041	
  1042	    runSimulation(state);
  1043	
  1044	    const double localSimDuration = (MPI_Wtime() - startSim) * 1000.0;
  1045	    double simDurationMs = 0.0;
  1046	    MPI_Reduce(&localSimDuration, &simDurationMs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, state.comm);
  1047	    const long simDuration = static_cast<long>(simDurationMs);
  1048	
  1049	    if (state.isRoot()) {
  1050	        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
  1051	        printf("\n");
  1052	    }
  1053	
  1054	    // Distance computation
  1055	    MPI_Barrier(state.comm);
  1056	    const double startDist = MPI_Wtime();
  1057	
  1058	    computeDistances(state);
  1059	
  1060	    const double localDistDuration = (MPI_Wtime() - startDist) * 1000.0;
  1061	    double distDurationMs = 0.0;
  1062	    MPI_Reduce(&localDistDuration, &distDurationMs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, state.comm);
  1063	    const long distDuration = static_cast<long>(distDurationMs);
  1064	
  1065	    int exitCode = 0;
  1066	    if (state.isRoot()) {
  1067	        printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
  1068	        printf("\n");
  1069	
  1070	        // Total time
  1071	        const long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
  1072	        printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);
  1073	
  1074	        // Performance metrics
  1075	        const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
  1076	        const size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
  1077	        const double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
  1078	        const double simOps = kijOps * static_cast<double>(t);
  1079	        const double distOps = static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(t) * static_cast<double>(t);
  1080	
  1081	        printf("\nPerformance:\n");
  1082	        printf("  MPI ranks: %d\n", state.numRanks);
  1083	        printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
  1084	        printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
  1085	        printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
  1086	        printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
  1087	        printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
  1088	        printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
  1089	
  1090	        // Rank zero owns a largest row block, so this is also the maximum
  1091	        // persistent numerical state on any rank.
  1092	        const size_t maxLocalRows = static_cast<size_t>(state.triangleCounts.front());
  1093	        const size_t memKij = maxLocalRows * n * sizeof(val_t);
  1094	        const size_t memTau = maxLocalRows * n * sizeof(delay_t);
  1095	        const size_t memRad = t * n * sizeof(val_t);
  1096	        const size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
  1097	        printf("  Maximum memory per rank: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
  1098	
  1099	        // Hash
  1100	        const uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
  1101	        printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
  1102	        printf("\n");
  1103	
  1104	        // Print results for external validation
  1105	        if (printResults) {
  1106	            std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
  1107	            print_results(distData, "Distances");
  1108	        }
  1109	    }
  1110	
  1111	    // Validation
  1112	    if (validate) {
  1113	        if (!validateResults(state)) {
  1114	            exitCode = 1;
  1115	        }
  1116	    }
  1117	
  1118	    MPI_Finalize();
  1119	    return exitCode;
  1120	}

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- Mandatory MPI build configuration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Balanced receiver-triangle row decomposition for form factors, delays, simulation, and distance calculations.
- \(O(N^2/P)\) per-rank matrix storage with compact 8-bit delays.
- Batched `MPI_Allgatherv` radiosity exchanges based on the minimum propagation delay.
- Original seeded random stream preserved exactly across rank counts.
- Root-only reporting and distributed validation reductions.

Verification:

- Clean warning-free release build.
- Address/undefined sanitizer run passed.
- Identical baseline result hashes for 1, 2, and 4 ranks.
- Edge case with 24 ranks and 20 triangles passed.
- 320-triangle compute time scaled from 1491 ms on one rank to 393 ms on four ranks (~3.8×).
- No new workspace files created.

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- Mandatory MPI build configuration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Balanced receiver-triangle row decomposition for form factors, delays, simulation, and distance calculations.
- \(O(N^2/P)\) per-rank matrix storage with compact 8-bit delays.
- Batched `MPI_Allgatherv` radiosity exchanges based on the minimum propagation delay.
- Original seeded random stream preserved exactly across rank counts.
- Root-only reporting and distributed validation reductions.
